You are at Raider's Ridge. A broken spine of scrub and stacked stone one hard walk east of the Tartarian Outskirts. Raider sign is cut into half the rocks — spiral-marks, ridge-signs, tallies — because this is where the border gangs stage, meet, and settle what the outposts will not allow inside their walls.
